<script src="/script.js"></script>
<script>
    (function () {
        const latestBtn = document.getElementById('downloadLatestBtn');
        const label = document.getElementById('latestDeviceLabel');
        const stableBadge = document.getElementById('nsoloStableBadge');

        const fallback = {
            version: 'v1.1.0',
            latestPage: 'https://github.com/NextPhasesDev/Nsolo/releases/latest',
            windows: {
                label: 'Windows (.exe, Java required for v1.1.0)',
                url: 'https://github.com/NextPhasesDev/Nsolo/releases/download/v1.1.0/Nsolo-v1.1.0.exe'
            },
            mac: {
                label: 'macOS (coming in v1.2.0)',
                url: ''
            },
            linux: {
                label: 'Linux (coming in v1.2.0)',
                url: ''
            },
            jar: {
                label: 'Universal Java JAR',
                url: 'https://github.com/NextPhasesDev/Nsolo/releases/download/v1.1.0/Nsolo.jar'
            }
        };

        const links = {
            windows: document.querySelector('[data-os-link="windows"]'),
            mac: document.querySelector('[data-os-link="mac"]'),
            linux: document.querySelector('[data-os-link="linux"]'),
            jar: document.querySelector('[data-os-link="jar"]')
        };

        function setLink(anchor, url, disabledText) {
            if (!anchor) return;
            if (url) {
                anchor.href = url;
                anchor.classList.remove('is-disabled');
                anchor.removeAttribute('aria-disabled');
                anchor.onclick = null;
            } else {
                anchor.href = '#';
                anchor.classList.add('is-disabled');
                anchor.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
                if (disabledText) anchor.querySelector('span').textContent = disabledText;
                anchor.onclick = function (e) { e.preventDefault(); };
            }
        }

        function detectDeviceProfile() {
            const ua = (navigator.userAgent || '').toLowerCase();
            const platform = (navigator.platform || '').toLowerCase();
            if (ua.indexOf('mac') >= 0 || platform.indexOf('mac') >= 0) return 'mac';
            if (ua.indexOf('linux') >= 0 || platform.indexOf('linux') >= 0) return 'linux';
            return 'windows';
        }

        function applyConfig(config) {
            const device = detectDeviceProfile();
            const preferred = config[device] || config.windows;

            if (label) label.textContent = preferred.label + ' - latest ' + config.version;
            if (stableBadge) stableBadge.textContent = 'Current Stable: ' + config.version;
            if (latestBtn) latestBtn.href = preferred.url || config.jar.url || config.latestPage;

            setLink(links.windows, config.windows.url);
            setLink(links.mac, config.mac.url, 'Coming Soon');
            setLink(links.linux, config.linux.url, 'Coming Soon');
            setLink(links.jar, config.jar.url);
        }

        applyConfig(fallback);

        const controller = new AbortController();
        const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 5000);

        fetch('https://api.github.com/repos/NextPhasesDev/Nsolo/releases/latest', {
            headers: { 'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json' },
            signal: controller.signal
        })
            .then(r => r.ok ? r.json() : Promise.reject(new Error('release fetch failed')))
            .then(release => {
                const assets = Array.isArray(release.assets) ? release.assets : [];
                const jarAsset = assets.find(a => /\.jar$/i.test(a.name || ''));
                const exeAsset = assets.find(a => /\.exe$/i.test(a.name || ''));
                const macAsset = assets.find(a => /\.(dmg|pkg|zip)$/i.test(a.name || '') && /mac|osx|darwin/i.test(a.name || ''));
                const linuxAsset = assets.find(a => /\.(appimage|deb|rpm|tar\.gz|tar\.xz)$/i.test(a.name || '') && /linux|ubuntu|debian|fedora|appimage/i.test(a.name || ''));

                const dynamic = {
                    version: (release.tag_name || fallback.version).replace(/^v/i, 'v'),
                    latestPage: release.html_url || fallback.latestPage,
                    windows: {
                        label: exeAsset ? 'Windows (.exe)' : fallback.windows.label,
                        url: exeAsset ? exeAsset.browser_download_url : fallback.windows.url
                    },
                    mac: {
                        label: macAsset ? 'macOS build' : fallback.mac.label,
                        url: macAsset ? macAsset.browser_download_url : fallback.mac.url
                    },
                    linux: {
                        label: linuxAsset ? 'Linux build' : fallback.linux.label,
                        url: linuxAsset ? linuxAsset.browser_download_url : fallback.linux.url
                    },
                    jar: {
                        label: jarAsset ? 'Universal Java JAR' : fallback.jar.label,
                        url: jarAsset ? jarAsset.browser_download_url : fallback.jar.url
                    }
                };

                applyConfig(dynamic);
            })
            .catch(() => {
                // Keep fallback links when API is unavailable.
            })
            .finally(() => {
                clearTimeout(timeoutId);
            });
    })();
</script>

<script src="/script.js"></script>
<script>
    (function () {
        const search = document.getElementById('gamesSearch');
        const cards = document.querySelectorAll('[data-game-card]');
        const chips = document.querySelectorAll('[data-game-filter]');
        if (!search || !cards.length) return;

        let activeFilter = 'all';

        function applyFilters() {
            const term = search.value.trim().toLowerCase();
            cards.forEach(card => {
                const title = (card.getAttribute('data-title') || '').toLowerCase();
                const categories = (card.getAttribute('data-categories') || '').toLowerCase();
                const searchMatch = !term || title.indexOf(term) >= 0 || categories.indexOf(term) >= 0;
                const categoryMatch = activeFilter === 'all' || categories.indexOf(activeFilter) >= 0;
                card.style.display = searchMatch && categoryMatch ? '' : 'none';
            });
        }

        search.addEventListener('input', applyFilters);
        chips.forEach(chip => {
            chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
                chips.forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                chip.classList.add('active');
                activeFilter = chip.getAttribute('data-game-filter') || 'all';
                applyFilters();
            });
        });
    })();
</script>

    /* ── CAROUSEL INIT ───────────────────────────────────── */
    var carousel = document.getElementById('npCarousel');
    if (!carousel) return;

    var track   = document.getElementById('npTrack');
    var dotsEl  = document.getElementById('npDots');
    var prevBtn = document.getElementById('npPrev');

    if (!track || !dotsEl || !prevBtn || !nextBtn) return;

    var current   = 0;
    var animating = false;
    var autoId    = null;
    var touchX    = 0;
    var N         = MEMBERS.length;

    /* Build cards */
    MEMBERS.forEach(function (m, i) {
        track.appendChild(buildCard(m, i));
    });

    /* Build dots */
    MEMBERS.forEach(function (m, i) {
        var d = document.createElement('button');
        d.className = 'np-dot';
        d.setAttribute('role', 'tab');
        d.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Go to ' + m.short);
        d.addEventListener('click', function () { goTo(i); });
        dotsEl.appendChild(d);
    });

    /* ── CARD BUILDER ──────────────────────────────────────── */
    function buildCard(m, idx) {
        var card = document.createElement('article');
        card.className = 'np-card';
        card.dataset.index  = idx;
        card.dataset.member = m.id;
        card.setAttribute('role', 'tabpanel');
        card.setAttribute('aria-label', m.name + ', ' + m.role);

        /* Particle spans */
        var particles = m.particles.slice(0, 8).map(function (sym) {
            var top  = (8  + Math.random() * 76).toFixed(1);
            var left = (4  + Math.random() * 86).toFixed(1);
            var dur  = (10 + Math.random() * 12).toFixed(1);
            var del  = (-(Math.random() * parseFloat(dur))).toFixed(2);
            var dx   = ((Math.random() - 0.5) * 28).toFixed(1);
            var dy   = ((Math.random() - 0.5) * 18).toFixed(1);
            return '<span class="np-particle" aria-hidden="true" style="'
                + 'top:' + top + '%;left:' + left + '%;'
                + 'animation-duration:' + dur + 's;'
                + 'animation-delay:' + del + 's;'
                + '--pdx:' + dx + 'px;--pdy:' + dy + 'px'
                + '">' + sym + '</span>';
        }).join('');

        /* Links */
        var links = m.links.map(function (l) {
            return '<a href="' + l.href + '" class="np-link">'
                + '<i class="' + l.icon + '" aria-hidden="true"></i>' + l.label + '</a>';
        });
        if (m.linkedin) {
            links.push('<a href="' + m.linkedin + '" class="np-link" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">'
                + '<i class="fab fa-linkedin" aria-hidden="true"></i>LinkedIn</a>');
        }

        /* Optional illustration image */
        var imgTag = m.artImg
            ? '<img src="' + m.artImg + '" alt="" loading="lazy">'
            : '';

        card.innerHTML = ''
            + '<div class="np-art" style="--art-s:' + m.artStart + ';--art-e:' + m.artEnd + '" aria-hidden="true">'
            +   imgTag
            +   particles
            +   '<i class="np-art-icon ' + m.icon + '"></i>'
            +   '<div class="np-art-shine"></div>'
            +   '<span class="np-art-label">' + m.short + '</span>'
            + '</div>'
            + '<div class="np-card-body">'
            +   '<span class="np-badge np-badge--' + m.badgeType + '">' + m.badge + '</span>'
            +   '<h3 class="np-name">' + m.name + '</h3>'
            +   '<p class="np-role-title">' + m.role + '</p>'
            +   '<p class="np-bio">' + m.bio + '</p>'
            +   '<div class="np-skills">' + m.skills.map(function (s) { return '<span>' + s + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
            +   (links.length ? '<div class="np-links">' + links.join('') + '</div>' : '')
            + '</div>';

        /* Clicking a side card navigates to it */
        card.addEventListener('click', function () {
            if (card.classList.contains('is-prev')) goTo(current - 1);
            if (card.classList.contains('is-next')) goTo(current + 1);
        });

        return card;
    }

    /* ── STATE UPDATE ─────────────────────────────────────── */
    function update() {
        var cards = track.querySelectorAll('.np-card');
        var dots  = dotsEl.querySelectorAll('.np-dot');

        cards.forEach(function (c, i) {
            c.classList.remove('is-active', 'is-prev', 'is-next');
            var d = ((i - current) % N + N) % N;
            if (d === 0)     c.classList.add('is-active');
            else if (d === N - 1) c.classList.add('is-prev');
            else if (d === 1)    c.classList.add('is-next');
        });

        dots.forEach(function (d, i) {
            d.classList.toggle('is-active', i === current);
            d.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(i === current));
        });

        carousel.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Team carousel: ' + MEMBERS[current].name);
    }

    /* ── NAVIGATION ───────────────────────────────────────── */
    function goTo(idx) {
        if (animating) return;
        var nxt = ((idx % N) + N) % N;
        if (nxt === current) return;
        animating = true;
        current = nxt;
        update();
        setTimeout(function () { animating = false; }, 620);
    }

    function advance() { goTo(current + 1); }
    function retreat() { goTo(current - 1); }

    prevBtn.addEventListener('click', retreat);
    nextBtn.addEventListener('click', advance);

    /* Touch/swipe */
    let startX = 0;
    let startTime = 0;

    carousel.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
        startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
        startTime = Date.now();
    }, { passive: true });

    carousel.addEventListener('touchend', e => {
        const dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - startX;
        const dt = Date.now() - startTime;

        const velocity = dx / dt;

        if (Math.abs(velocity) > 0.5) {
            velocity > 0 ? retreat() : advance();
        } else if (Math.abs(dx) > 50) {
            dx > 0 ? retreat() : advance();
        }
    }, { passive: true });

    /* Keyboard */
    carousel.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
        if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); advance(); }
        if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft')  { e.preventDefault(); retreat(); }
    });

    /* Auto-advance */
    function startAuto() { autoId = setInterval(advance, 5500); }
    function stopAuto()  { clearInterval(autoId); }

    carousel.addEventListener('mouseenter', stopAuto);
    carousel.addEventListener('mouseleave', startAuto);
    carousel.addEventListener('focusin',    stopAuto);
    carousel.addEventListener('focusout',   startAuto);

    update();
    startAuto();

    /* ── SECTION BACKGROUND ───────────────────────────────── */
    initTeamBg();

    function initTeamBg() {
        var canvas = document.getElementById('npTeamCanvas');
        if (!canvas) return;

        var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        var W = 0, H = 0;
        var mouseX = 0.5, mouseY = 0.5;
        var tgtX   = 0.5, tgtY   = 0.5;

        var section = canvas.closest
            ? canvas.closest('.np-team-wrapper')
            : canvas.parentNode;

        function resize() {
            var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
            W = canvas.offsetWidth;
            H = canvas.offsetHeight;
            canvas.width  = Math.round(W * dpr);
            canvas.height = Math.round(H * dpr);
            ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
        }

        /* Mouse tracking */
        if (section) {
            section.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
                var r = section.getBoundingClientRect();
                tgtX = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width;
                tgtY = (e.clientY - r.top)  / r.height;
            });
            section.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
                tgtX = 0.5; tgtY = 0.5;
            });
        }

        /* Device orientation fallback for mobile */
        if ('DeviceOrientationEvent' in window) {
            window.addEventListener('deviceorientation', function (e) {
                if (e.gamma == null || e.beta == null) return;
                tgtX = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.5 + e.gamma / 45));
                tgtY = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.5 + e.beta  / 45));
            }, { passive: true });
        }

        /* Soft blobs */
        var blobs = [
            { x: 18, y: 25, r: 130, vx:  0.05, vy:  0.04, h: 200, a: 0.1  },
            { x: 75, y: 60, r: 115, vx: -0.04, vy:  0.05, h: 170, a: 0.09 },
            { x: 45, y: 80, r: 140, vx:  0.03, vy: -0.04, h: 185, a: 0.08 },
            { x: 85, y: 20, r: 100, vx: -0.05, vy:  0.03, h: 210, a: 0.08 },
            { x: 30, y: 50, r: 120, vx:  0.04, vy: -0.05, h: 195, a: 0.09 },
            { x: 60, y: 40, r:  90, vx: -0.03, vy:  0.04, h: 165, a: 0.07 }
        ];

        function draw() {
            if (!W || !H) { requestAnimationFrame(draw); return; }
            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

            /* Smooth mouse follow */
            mouseX += (tgtX - mouseX) * 0.04;
            mouseY += (tgtY - mouseY) * 0.04;

            var ox = (mouseX - 0.5) * -28;
            var oy = (mouseY - 0.5) * -18;

            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(ox, oy);

            blobs.forEach(function (b) {
                b.x += b.vx;
                b.y += b.vy;
                if (b.x < -15) b.x = 115;
                if (b.x > 115) b.x = -15;
                if (b.y < -15) b.y = 115;
                if (b.y > 115) b.y = -15;

                var gx = (b.x / 100) * (W + 60) - 30;
                var gy = (b.y / 100) * (H + 60) - 30;

                var g = ctx.createRadialGradient(gx, gy, 0, gx, gy, b.r);
                g.addColorStop(0, 'hsla(' + b.h + ',65%,38%,' + b.a + ')');
                g.addColorStop(1, 'transparent');

                ctx.fillStyle = g;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.arc(gx, gy, b.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ctx.fill();
            });

            ctx.restore();
            requestAnimationFrame(draw);
        }

        resize();
        window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
        draw();
    }

    /* ── MAGNETIC BUTTONS (site-wide) ─────────────────────── */
    /*
     * This adds a subtle magnetic pull to all .cta-button elements.
     * Safe to call multiple times — uses a data attribute flag to prevent
     * double-binding. You can also move this to script.js if preferred.
     */
    initMagneticButtons();

    function initMagneticButtons() {
        var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
        if (reducedMotion) return;

        document.querySelectorAll('.cta-button:not([data-magnetic])').forEach(function (btn) {
            btn.dataset.magnetic = '1';

            btn.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
                var rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
                var cx = rect.left + rect.width  / 2;
                var cy = rect.top  + rect.height / 2;
                var dx = (e.clientX - cx) * 0.28;
                var dy = (e.clientY - cy) * 0.28;
                btn.style.transform = 'translate(' + dx + 'px,' + dy + 'px)';
            });

            btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
                btn.style.transform = '';
            });
        });
    }

    /* ── GLOBAL PARALLAX for moonCanvas ──────────────────── */
    /*
     * Gives the existing background animation a "looking around"
     * depth effect. The canvas shifts slightly opposite to cursor
     * movement — creates the illusion of a 3D scene you're panning.
     * scale(1.06) ensures no edges are visible during the shift.
     *
     * Runs only on desktop (pointer: fine) to avoid drain on mobile.
     */
    if (!('ontouchstart' in window) && window.matchMedia('(pointer: fine)').matches) {
        initGlobalParallax();
    }

    function initGlobalParallax() {
        var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
        if (reducedMotion) return;

        var moonCanvas = document.getElementById('moonCanvas');
        if (!moonCanvas) return;

        var px = 0.5, py = 0.5;  /* smoothed position */
        var tx = 0.5, ty = 0.5;  /* target */
        var rafId = null;
        var active = false;

        document.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
            tx = e.clientX / window.innerWidth;
            ty = e.clientY / window.innerHeight;
            if (!active) { active = true; rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }
        }, { passive: true });

        function tick() {
            px += (tx - px) * 0.06;
            py += (ty - py) * 0.06;

            var mx = (px - 0.5) * -18;
            var my = (py - 0.5) * -12;

            moonCanvas.style.transform = 'translate(' + mx + 'px,' + my + 'px) scale(1.06)';

            var d = Math.abs(tx - px) + Math.abs(ty - py);
            if (d < 0.0008) {
                active = false;
                cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
            } else {
                rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
            }
        }
    }

    /* ── LEGACY PANEL (graceful no-op if elements absent) ── */
    (function () {
        var panel  = document.getElementById('teamDetailPanel');
        if (!panel) return;
        var overlay = document.getElementById('teamDetailOverlay');
        var close   = document.getElementById('detailClose');
        if (!overlay || !close) return;
        function closePanel() {
            overlay.classList.remove('active');
            panel.classList.remove('active');
        }
        close.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); closePanel(); });
        overlay.addEventListener('click', closePanel);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
            if (e.key === 'Escape' && panel.classList.contains('active')) closePanel();
        });
    })();

})();

# AGENTS: How to work with this codebase (for automated coding agents)

Purpose: Give an AI or coding agent the minimal, targeted knowledge to be productive immediately.

Checklist (what I'll cover)
- Big-picture architecture and why it is organized this way
- Critical developer workflows (local preview, quick debug commands)
- Project-specific conventions and patterns (where to change behavior)
- Integration points and external dependencies to be careful with

Quick summary
- Static, framework-less website (HTML + CSS + one large vanilla `script.js`). No build step.
- Primary entry: `index.html` (other pages mirror the same structure). Global behavior is centralized in `script.js`.

Architecture & major components
- Root HTML pages: `index.html`, `services.html`, `portfolio.html`, `about.html`, `contact.html`, etc. These are static files served as-is.
- Master JS: `script.js` — single large file (client-side app shell) that implements:
  - DOM / UX behaviors (theme, nav, mobile drawer, CTA animations)
  - Pricing & region detection (see `getRegionConfig`, `detectRegionByLocale`, `initPricingCurrency`)
  - Form handling and submission to Formspree (endpoint hard-coded in `script.js` around the contact form submit)
  - Fragment loading: `loadNavbar()` fetches `navbar.html`, `loadFooter()` fetches `footer.html` and falls back to the inline `fallbackFooter` string when fetch fails.
  - Canvas background animations for each page (many init*Animation functions)
- HTML fragments: `navbar.html` and `footer.html` are fetched and injected at runtime. Pages may also include a `navbarMount` element where `navbar.html` is inserted.
- Static assets / pages: `style.css`, `Logo_transparent.webp`, `games/` subfolder (nested route examples e.g. `/games/nsolo/`).

Developer workflows (how to run / test / debug)
- No package manager or build step. To preview locally:
  - Recommended (fast): run a static server from the project root:
    - PowerShell: `python -m http.server 8000 ; Start-Process http://localhost:8000` (if Python available)
    - Or install & use VS Code Live Server extension and open the workspace.
  - Then open `http://localhost:8000/index.html` in a browser.
- Editing + debugging:
  - All interactive logic lives in `script.js`. Use browser DevTools (Sources) to set breakpoints inside `script.js` (it's not split into modules nor sourcemapped).
  - Search `script.js` for a function name to find the behavior to change. Example: to adjust how pricing is chosen search `detectRegionByLocale` or `getPricingTierValues`.
  - To change the contact form endpoint update the endpoint string near `const endpoint = 'https://formspree.io/f/xgoldrwl';` (also update `.env` to reflect the new value for documentation parity).

Project-specific conventions & patterns (explicit examples)
- Single-file logic: The team places almost all runtime behavior into `script.js`. New features should follow the same pattern unless you intentionally refactor into modules.
- DOM-first coordination: components communicate via DOM (IDs, attributes, data-*). Examples:
  - data-tier on pricing elements (used in `updatePricingDisplay`) — update these attributes when altering markup.
  - `data-showcase` on `.pricing-tier` to map carousel slides to tiers in `initShowcaseCarousel`.
  - `data-member` on `.clickable-team` cards (used in `initTeamDetails`).
  - `form.dataset.preselectedPlan` is used to preselect budget options from URL `plan` param.
- Progressive enhancement & graceful fallback:
  - `loadFooter()` attempts fetch('footer.html') and falls back to `fallbackFooter` string embedded in `script.js` — follow that pattern for other fragment injections.
  - Feature detection used widely (e.g., `IntersectionObserver`, `prefers-reduced-motion`, touch detection). Keep those checks when modifying animations.
- Asset resolution: `resolveSitePath(path)` ensures a leading slash; pages assume absolute-like paths (leading `/`). When adding assets use the same path style.

Integration points & external dependencies
- Formspree (contact form): endpoint in `script.js` (see contact form submit logic). `.env` contains a placeholder `FORMSPREE_ENDPOINT` but the runtime uses the string in `script.js`.
- Google Analytics: `gtag.js` is included in `index.html` (G-5T40D6EZG4). If you change GA, update the ID in the markup.
- Google Fonts & Font Awesome: loaded via external CDN in HTML head (`fonts.googleapis.com` and `cdnjs.cloudflare.com`). Keep fallback `<noscript>` links present.
- Social links & metadata: `index.html`, `footer.html` and `script.js` contain hard-coded social links; update them in both HTML and `.env` for clarity.
- Netlify: README notes Netlify auto-deploy on `main`. No CI scripts in this repo—deploy is the standard static deployment (push -> Netlify build which is usually a direct publish for static folders).

What to change vs what to preserve
- Change in `script.js` when you need to modify runtime UX, pricing rules (budget maps at top of file), or contact form behavior.
- Modify HTML (pages or fragments) for structure/content changes. Preserve expected IDs and data-* attributes used by `script.js` (e.g., `contactForm`, `pricingRegion`, `projectType`, `navbarMount`).
- Modify `style.css` for visual updates. CSS variables are used for theming; theme toggle updates `document.documentElement.dataset.theme` and `localStorage.theme`.

Quick pointers for common tasks (examples)
- Change Formspree: edit `script.js` line with `const endpoint = 'https://formspree.io/f/xgoldrwl';` (search `formspree` to find it). Also update `.env.FORMSPREE_ENDPOINT` for documentation.
- Add a new pricing tier: update the budget maps near the top of `script.js` (e.g., `budgetMapDefault` / `budgetMapSystems`) and the `pricingDisplayData` objects.
- Add a new page-specific canvas animation: add `initYourAnimation(canvas, ctx)` and plug it into `initMoonAnimation`'s page selection.
- Make navbar static (server-side): replace client-side fetch + mount by including the content directly in each page — remember the JS `loadNavbar` will try to mount from `#navbarMount` if present.

Notes for automated agents
- Do not assume a JS build step. Tests or linters are not present.
- Always preserve DOM IDs and data-* attributes unless you update `script.js` accordingly.
- When editing `script.js`, run a quick local static server and verify interactions that depend on runtime injection (navbar/footer fetch) because editing fragment files may change runtime DOM.
- Search for the exact string you need to change in `script.js` — functions are grouped and well-named (e.g. `initContactForm`, `initPricingCurrency`, `initShowcaseCarousel`).

Files to review first (entry points)
- `index.html` — site shell & metadata
- `script.js` — master behavior file (largest single source of runtime logic)
- `style.css` — visual styling and theme variables
- `navbar.html`, `footer.html` — fragments injected at runtime
- `.env` — documentation of external endpoints (not automatically loaded at runtime)

If you need help making a specific change, tell me the exact behavior to alter (e.g., "change Formspree endpoint", "add a pricing tier", "convert to modular JS") and I'll modify the files and run quick local checks.

    <section class="faq-section" id="faq-section">
        <div class="faq-container">
            <h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>
            <p class="faq-intro">Find answers to common questions about our services, pricing, and process.</p>

            <div class="faq-grid">
                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>How long does a web development project take with NextPhases?</summary>
                    <p>A straightforward business website typically takes 4 to 6 weeks from signed agreement to delivery. A full web application with authentication, database, and multiple user roles is usually 8 to 16 weeks. We break every project into clear milestones and communicate progress at each stage.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>Do NextPhases work with international clients?</summary>
                    <p>Yes. We work with clients in Zambia, Zimbabwe, South Africa, the UK, Europe, the US, Canada, and across Africa and the Middle East. Our entire workflow is remote-friendly. We use email, WhatsApp, and video calls to stay aligned regardless of timezone.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>Can NextPhases redesign or rebuild an existing website?</summary>
                    <p>Yes, and it's one of the most common requests we handle. We can audit your existing site or app, identify what's working and what isn't, then redesign it while preserving anything worth keeping. We'll tell you what's worth keeping before recommending a full restart.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>Will my website work on mobile phones and tablets?</summary>
                    <p>Every website and application we build is mobile-first by default. We design for the smallest screen first, then scale up. We test on real devices across multiple screen sizes and browsers before delivery. Given that most traffic in Africa comes from mobile devices, this is non-negotiable for us.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>What technologies does NextPhases use?</summary>
                    <p>We choose the right tool for the job rather than forcing every project into one stack. We're experienced with HTML, CSS, JavaScript, React, Node.js, Python, Java, PostgreSQL, MySQL, REST APIs, and deployment on Netlify, Render, Railway, and cloud platforms.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>Do you provide maintenance and support after launch?</summary>
                    <p>Yes. Every project includes a post-launch support window during which we handle any bugs or issues that appear. Beyond that, we offer ongoing maintenance arrangements covering security updates, performance checks, feature additions, and content changes.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>Can NextPhases build custom business systems?</summary>
                    <p>Yes. Custom business systems like dashboards, staff management tools, inventory trackers, booking systems, and workflow automation are a core part of what we do. If you have a business process that relies on spreadsheets, paper, or disconnected tools, we can build something better.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>How much does a project cost?</summary>
                    <p>Pricing depends on scope, complexity, and timeline. We use regional pricing to keep our services accessible. We're transparent about pricing from the first conversation and provide detailed estimates before any work starts. We require a 30% deposit after a prototype is approved, with the remaining 70% on final delivery.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>Does NextPhases help with SEO and performance?</summary>
                    <p>Yes. Every website we build includes SEO foundations as standard: semantic HTML structure, proper heading hierarchy, fast load times, mobile optimization, descriptive meta tags, and structured data markup. We don't charge extra for these—they're part of building a website correctly.</p>
                </details>

                <details class="faq-item">
                    <summary>How do I start a project with NextPhases?</summary>
                    <p>Reach out through our contact form or directly on WhatsApp. Tell us what you're building, who it's for, and roughly when you need it. We'll respond within 24 to 48 hours and schedule a free discovery call. No commitment required at that stage.</p>
                </details>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

# NextPhases

> Software development company based in Lusaka, Zambia. We build websites, mobile apps, custom software systems, and technical consulting for businesses in Zambia, Africa, the UK, and internationally.

## Company

NextPhases is founded by a team of software engineers and digital strategists committed to delivering high-quality, scalable solutions for businesses at every stage of growth. We specialize in transforming business challenges into elegant technical solutions, combining strategic thinking with solid engineering practices. Our team has experience across Africa and internationally, helping startups and enterprises launch products that matter.

## Team

NextPhases is founded and led by experienced software engineers and digital strategists:

- **Founding Lead, Full-Stack Development & Architecture** – Expert in modern web technologies (React, Node.js, Python) with years of real-world experience building scalable systems
-  **Technical Strategy & System Design** – Specialist in infrastructure, database optimization, and long-term technical planning for growing products
- **Mobile Development** – iOS and Android expert with experience shipping cross-platform apps to production
- **UX/UI Design & Product** – Creates intuitive interfaces and leads the user experience vision for all projects
- **DevOps & Infrastructure** – Ensures reliability, performance, and security across all deployments
- **Client Leadership & Project Management** – Keeps clients informed, teams aligned, and projects moving

Each team member brings real-world experience from working on diverse projects across industries, with a shared commitment to clean code, continuous learning, and client success.

## Services

- Web Development: business websites, landing pages, web applications, e-commerce, performance optimization
- App Development: Android, iOS, cross-platform mobile apps, web apps, internal tools
- Technical Consulting: tech stack selection, digital transformation, architecture planning, security
- Custom Software: dashboards, automation systems, business management tools, SaaS products

## Products

- Nsolo: a digital adaptation of a traditional Zambian mancala-style board game
- ExamGuard: exam management and invigilation platform for schools and universities

## Pricing

Regional pricing model. ZMW for Zambia. ZAR for South Africa. GBP/EUR for UK/Europe. USD for US, Canada, and international. Contact for a quote.

## Contact

Email: info@nextphases.dev
Website: https://nextphases.dev
Location: Lusaka, Zambia

## Resources

[services.html](https://nextphases.dev/services.html): Full services listing with pricing regions
[portfolio.html](https://nextphases.dev/portfolio.html): Our products and past work
[contact.html](https://nextphases.dev/contact.html): Get a quote or book a discovery call

    <section class="legal-content">
        <div class="legal-container">
            <p class="legal-updated">Last updated: March 2026</p>

            <p>NextPhases ("we", "us", "our") is a software development company based in Lusaka, Zambia. This Privacy Policy explains how we collect, use, store, and protect your personal information when you use our website, services, or products. We are committed to handling your data responsibly and transparently.</p>

            <div style="background: var(--card-bg); border-left: 3px solid var(--primary-teal); padding: 1rem 1.5rem; border-radius: 0 8px 8px 0; margin: 1.5rem 0;">
                <p style="margin: 0;"><strong>Plain language summary:</strong> We collect only what we need, we never sell your data, we don't use invasive tracking, and you can ask us to delete your information at any time. Read on for the full details.</p>
            </div>

            <h2>1. Who This Policy Applies To</h2>
            <p>This policy applies to:</p>
            <ul>
                <li>Visitors to nextphases.dev</li>
                <li>Individuals who contact us via our website or email</li>
                <li>Clients engaged for software development services</li>
                <li>Users of NextPhases products, including Nsolo (our downloadable desktop game and future web version) and ExamGuard (our exam management platform, when available)</li>
                <li>Users of any future NextPhases platform features, including account systems or community tools</li>
            </ul>

            <h2>2. Information We Collect</h2>

            <p><strong>2.1 Information you give us directly</strong></p>
            <ul>
                <li>Name, email address, and company name submitted via our contact form</li>
                <li>Project details including scope, budget, and timeline</li>
                <li>Any additional information you choose to share in messages or during project engagements</li>
            </ul>

            <p><strong>2.2 Information collected automatically</strong></p>
            <ul>
                <li>Your browser's light/dark mode preference, stored in local storage on your device. This never leaves your browser.</li>
            </ul>

            <p><strong>2.3 Information we do NOT collect</strong></p>
            <ul>
                <li>We do not use advertising cookies or cross-site trackers</li>
                <li>We do not use browser fingerprinting or covert identification techniques</li>
                <li>We do not collect payment card details directly (payments are handled via third-party invoicing tools)</li>
            </ul>

            <h2>3. How We Use Your Information</h2>
            <ul>
                <li>To respond to your enquiries and project requests</li>
                <li>To deliver the services or products you have engaged us for</li>
                <li>To send project-related updates (only to active clients)</li>
                <li>To improve our website, products, and services</li>
                <li>To comply with legal obligations where required</li>
            </ul>
            <p>We will never send you unsolicited marketing emails. We will never share your information with third-party advertisers.</p>

            <h2>4. Third-Party Services We Use</h2>
            <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 1rem 0; font-size: 0.9rem;">
                <thead>

            <h2>5. Data Sharing</h2>
            <p>We do not sell, rent, or trade your personal information. We may share data only in the following circumstances:</p>
            <ul>
                <li>When required by law, court order, or legal process in Zambia or another applicable jurisdiction</li>
                <li>With service providers who help us operate our website and services, strictly for that purpose and bound by confidentiality obligations</li>
                <li>With your explicit consent</li>
            </ul>

            <h2>6. Data Storage and Security</h2>
            <p>Your data is handled using industry-standard security practices. All traffic to and from nextphases.dev is encrypted via SSL/TLS. Contact form data is transmitted over HTTPS through EmailJS's secure infrastructure.</p>
            <p>We retain your information only as long as necessary to fulfil the purpose for which it was collected, or as required by applicable law. Client project data is retained for a minimum of two years after project completion for record-keeping purposes.</p>
            <p>While we take reasonable precautions, no internet transmission is completely secure. We encourage you not to share sensitive information (such as passwords or financial details) via our contact form.</p>

            <h2>7. International Users</h2>
            <p>NextPhases is based in Lusaka, Zambia. If you access our website or services from outside Zambia, including from the European Union or United Kingdom, your data may be transferred to and processed in Zambia. By using our services, you acknowledge this transfer.</p>
            <p>For users in the EU or UK, we process your data based on the following lawful bases under GDPR and UK GDPR: your consent (where given), the performance of a contract with you, and our legitimate interests in operating and improving our services.</p>

            <h2>8. Your Rights</h2>
            <p>Regardless of your location, you have the right to:</p>
            <ul>
                <li>Request access to the personal data we hold about you</li>
                <li>Request correction of inaccurate data</li>
                <li>Request deletion of your data ("right to be forgotten")</li>
                <li>Withdraw consent for data processing at any time</li>
                <li>Request a portable copy of your data</li>
                <li>Object to how we use your data</li>
            </ul>
            <p>To exercise any of these rights, email us at <a href="mailto:info@nextphases.dev">info@nextphases.dev</a>. We will respond within 30 days.</p>

            <h2>9. Children's Privacy</h2>
            <p>Our professional services (web development, consulting) are not directed to individuals under the age of 16. We do not knowingly collect personal information from anyone under 16. If you are a parent or guardian and believe your child has provided us with personal information, contact us at <a href="mailto:info@nextphases.dev">info@nextphases.dev</a> and we will delete it promptly.</p>
            <p>Nsolo is a culturally focused digital adaptation of a traditional Zambian board game, available as a downloadable desktop application (Windows). It has no age restriction. <strong>Nsolo does not collect, transmit, or store any personal information.</strong> It requires no account creation, has no internet connectivity, performs no telemetry or analytics, and involves no in-app purchases. All game data is stored locally on the user's own device and is never sent to NextPhases or any third party.</p>

            <h2>10. Cookies and Local Storage</h2>
            <ul>
                <li><strong>Theme preference:</strong> Stored in your browser's local storage to remember your light/dark mode choice. This data stays on your device and is never transmitted to us.</li>
                <li><strong>No tracking cookies:</strong> We do not use advertising cookies, session tracking cookies, or any third-party analytics cookies.</li>
            </ul>

            <h2>11. Our Applications and Products</h2>
            <p>NextPhases builds digital products alongside our client services. This section describes the data practices of our current products. New products will be listed here as they are released.</p>

            <p><strong>Nsolo (Desktop Application)</strong></p>
            <p>The Nsolo application (Windows .exe / .jar) is a fully offline, standalone program. It does not connect to the internet during normal operation.</p>
            <ul>
                <li><strong>No data collection:</strong> Nsolo does not collect, log, or transmit any personal information, usage data, device identifiers, or analytics of any kind.</li>
                <li><strong>No network requests:</strong> The application makes no outbound network connections. It does not ping any server, send crash reports, or check for updates automatically.</li>
                <li><strong>Local storage only:</strong> Any data saved (such as game preferences) is stored exclusively on the user's device and is never transmitted to NextPhases or any third party.</li>
                <li><strong>No third-party SDKs:</strong> The application does not include advertising SDKs, analytics libraries, or tracking frameworks.</li>
                <li><strong>Open source:</strong> The Nsolo source code is publicly available for independent verification at <a href="https://github.com/NextPhasesDev" target="_blank" rel="noopener">github.com/NextPhasesDev</a>.</li>
            </ul>
            <p>If your security software flags the Nsolo installer, this is a false positive caused by the application being unsigned (code signing certificates are a paid service we have not yet acquired). The application is safe. You can verify the source code via the GitHub link above.</p>

            <p><strong>ExamGuard (Coming Soon)</strong></p>
            <p>ExamGuard is an exam management platform for schools and institutions. When available, it will be governed by its own institutional licence agreement and a dedicated data processing addendum. This section will be updated when ExamGuard enters public availability.</p>

            <h2>12. Changes to This Policy</h2>
            <p>We may update this Privacy Policy from time to time. When we do, we will update the "Last updated" date at the top of this page and, where changes are significant, notify active clients directly. Continued use of our website after changes have been posted constitutes acknowledgement of the updated policy.</p>

            <h2>13. Contact</h2>
            <p>If you have any questions, concerns, or requests about this Privacy Policy or how we handle your data, please contact us:</p>

                <li><strong>Location:</strong> Lusaka, Zambia</li>

# NextPhases

Professional software development studio based in Lusaka, Zambia, delivering scalable solutions for startups, businesses, and enterprises worldwide.

🌐 **Visit:** [nextphases.dev](https://nextphases.dev)

---

## What We Do

We specialize in:
- **Web Development** - Full-stack, responsive, scalable web applications
- **Mobile App Development** - iOS & Android solutions
- **System Development** - Enterprise software architecture
- **Technical Consulting** - Expert guidance for digital transformation

We work with clients across **Zambia, Southern Africa, Europe, UK, and North America**.

---

## About This Repository

This is the source code for the NextPhases website. The site is built with clean, modern technology and is production-ready.

### Built With

- **HTML5 & CSS3** - Semantic, responsive markup
- **Vanilla JavaScript** - No frameworks, lightweight & fast
- **Modern UI/UX** - Dark/light mode, smooth animations, accessibility-first
- **Form Backend** - Formspree integration for client inquiries
- **Deployment** - Netlify with automatic SSL

### Key Features

- 📱 Fully responsive across all devices
- 🌓 Dark and light theme support
- 🌍 Multi-region pricing (auto-detects currency)
- ♿ Accessible design (WCAG compliant)
- ⚡ Fast load times with no external dependencies
- 🔒 Privacy-focused, secure form handling
- 🌐 SEO optimized with sitemap & structured data

---

## Getting Started

### View the Site

Open `index.html` in any modern browser. No build step required.

### Development

```bash
# No dependencies to install
# Simply open files locally and refresh browser after changes
```

### Deployment

The site is hosted on Netlify and auto-deploys on every push to `main`.

```
index.html          Homepage
services.html       Services & pricing
portfolio.html      Project showcase
about.html          Team & values
contact.html        Get in touch
testimonials.html   Client testimonials
privacy.html        Privacy policy
terms.html          Terms of service
```

 - 2026-05-16: `services.html` content expanded — updated service descriptions (Web Development, App Development, Technical Consulting) and added a new "How We Work" section describing the 6-step project process. Existing features lists retained.
	 - 2026-05-16: `services.html` FAQ section added with 8 frequently asked questions and answers; content edited for clarity and regional accuracy. No changes to nav/footer/scripts.
					 - 2026-05-16: `services.html` globe enhancement — added overlay canvas and connection-arc renderer in `services.js` to animate subtle arcs originating from Zambia to other pricing regions. No changes to existing globe interactions.
